## Analytics

### telemetry.getTrace

`analytics.telemetry.getTrace`

Get trace spans

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.analytics.telemetry.getTrace({ organizationId: 'b3c1f0a2-7e4d-4c9b-8a6f-1d2e3f4a5b6c', sandboxId: '0f9a3c2e-5d6b-4a1f-9c8e-2b7d4e6a1c3f', traceId: '4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organizationId` | `string` | Yes | Organization ID |
| `sandboxId` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID |
| `traceId` | `string` | Yes | Trace ID |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  durationMs?: number | null,
  parentSpanId?: string | null,
  spanAttributes?: Record<string, string> | null,
  spanId?: string | null,
  spanName?: string | null,
  statusCode?: string | null,
  statusMessage?: string | null,
  timestamp?: string | null,
  traceId?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### telemetry.logs

`analytics.telemetry.logs`

Get sandbox logs

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.analytics.telemetry.logs({
	organizationId: 'b3c1f0a2-7e4d-4c9b-8a6f-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	sandboxId: '0f9a3c2e-5d6b-4a1f-9c8e-2b7d4e6a1c3f',
	from: '2026-11-01T00:00:00Z',
	to: '2026-11-27T00:00:00Z',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organizationId` | `string` | Yes | Organization ID |
| `sandboxId` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID |
| `from` | `string` | Yes | Start time (RFC3339) |
| `to` | `string` | Yes | End time (RFC3339) |
| `severity` | `string` | No | Comma-separated severity levels |
| `search` | `string` | No | Search text (ILIKE) |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Page size |
| `offset` | `number` | No | Offset |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  body?: string | null,
  logAttributes?: Record<string, string> | null,
  resourceAttributes?: Record<string, string> | null,
  serviceName?: string | null,
  severityNumber?: number | null,
  severityText?: string | null,
  spanId?: string | null,
  timestamp?: string | null,
  traceId?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### telemetry.metrics

`analytics.telemetry.metrics`

Get sandbox metrics

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.analytics.telemetry.metrics({
	organizationId: 'b3c1f0a2-7e4d-4c9b-8a6f-1d2e3f4a5b6c',

	to: '2026-11-27T00:00:00Z',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organizationId` | `string` | Yes | Organization ID |
| `sandboxId` | `string` | Yes | Sandbox ID |
| `from` | `string` | Yes | Start time (RFC3339) |
| `to` | `string` | Yes | End time (RFC3339) |
| `metricNames` | `string` | No | Comma-separated metric names |

### usage.aggregated

`analytics.usage.aggregated`

## Api Keys

### create

`apiKeys.create`

Create API key

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.apiKeys.create({
	name: 'my-sandbox',
	permissions: ['write:registries'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The name of the API key |
| `permissions` | `(write:registries \| delete:registries \| write:snapshots \| delete:snapshots \| write:sandboxes \| delete:sandboxes \| read:volumes \| write:volumes \| delete:volumes \| write:regions \| delete:regions \| read:runners \| write:runners \| delete:runners \| read:audit_logs \| manage:api_keys \| manage:secrets \| read:limits \| manage:sso \| read:billing \| manage:billing)[]` | Yes | The list of organization resource permissions explicitly assigned to the API key |
| `expiresAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the API key expires |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the API key |
| `value` | `string \| null` | No | The API key value |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the API key was created |
| `permissions` | `string[] \| null` | No | The list of organization resource permissions assigned to the API key |
| `expiresAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the API key expires |



---

### delete

`apiKeys.delete`

Delete API key

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.apiKeys.delete({ name: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Daytona accepted the call; it answered with no body. |



---

### getCurrent

`apiKeys.getCurrent`

Get current API key's details

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.apiKeys.getCurrent({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the API key |
| `value` | `string \| null` | No | The masked API key value |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the API key was created |
| `permissions` | `string[] \| null` | No | The list of organization resource permissions assigned to the API key |
| `lastUsedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the API key was last used |
| `expiresAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the API key expires |
| `userId` | `string \| null` | No | The user ID of the user who created the API key |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID associated with the API key |



---

### list

`apiKeys.list`

List API keys

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.apiKeys.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  name?: string | null,
  value?: string | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  permissions?: string[] | null,
  lastUsedAt?: string | null | null,
  expiresAt?: string | null | null,
  userId?: string | null,
  organizationId?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Audit

### getOrganizationLogs

`audit.getOrganizationLogs`

Get audit logs for organization

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.audit.getOrganizationLogs({ organizationId: 'b3c1f0a2-7e4d-4c9b-8a6f-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organizationId` | `string` | Yes | Organization ID |
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number of the results |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Number of results per page |
| `from` | `string` | No | Deprecated alias for `createdAt[gte]`. From date (ISO 8601 format). |
| `to` | `string` | No | Deprecated alias for `createdAt[lte]`. To date (ISO 8601 format). |
| `nextToken` | `string` | No | Token for cursor-based pagination. When provided, takes precedence over page parameter. |
| `id` | `object` | No | — |
| `actorId` | `object` | No | — |
| `actorEmail` | `object` | No | — |
| `actorApiKeyPrefix` | `object` | No | — |
| `actorApiKeySuffix` | `object` | No | — |
| `action` | `object` | No | — |
| `targetType` | `object` | No | — |
| `targetId` | `object` | No | — |
| `statusCode` | `object` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `object` | No | — |
| `actorPartner` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="id full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: string,
  not?: string,
  in?: string[],
  notIn?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="actorId full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: string,
  not?: string,
  in?: string[],
  notIn?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="actorEmail full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: string,
  not?: string,
  in?: string[],
  notIn?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="actorApiKeyPrefix full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: string,
  not?: string,
  in?: string[],
  notIn?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="actorApiKeySuffix full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: string,
  not?: string,
  in?: string[],
  notIn?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="action full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: string,
  not?: string,
  in?: string[],
  notIn?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="targetType full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: string,
  not?: string,
  in?: string[],
  notIn?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="targetId full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: string,
  not?: string,
  in?: string[],
  notIn?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="statusCode full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: number,

  gte?: number,

```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="createdAt full type">

```ts
{
  gte?: string,
  lte?: string,
  gt?: string,
  lt?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="actorPartner full type">

```ts
{
  eq?: string,
  not?: string,
```

**Output**

| `page` | `number \| null` | No | — |

### getScenarios

`audit.getScenarios`

Get supported audit log scenarios

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.audit.getScenarios({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `targets` | `object[] \| null` | No | Supported audit log scenarios grouped by target type. Derived at runtime from the audited routes and system events, sorted by target type. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="targets full type">

```ts
{
  targetType?: string | null,
  actions?: string[] | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Config

### get

`config.get`

Get config

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.config.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `version` | `string \| null` | No | Daytona version |
| `buildSha` | `string \| null` | No | Commit sha of the source the app was built from |
| `posthog` | `object \| null` | No | PostHog configuration |
| `oidc` | `object \| null` | No | OIDC configuration |
| `ssoOidc` | `object \| null` | No | OIDC configuration for org-SSO logins (Daytona Auth issuer). Present only when the secondary (org-SSO) issuer is configured; the dashboard uses it as its authority when entered via an organization SSO link. |
| `forcedFeatureFlags` | `string[] \| null` | No | Feature flags forced on for this deployment regardless of PostHog targeting. Lets environments without PostHog (previews, local dev) enable flag-gated dashboard features. |
| `linkedAccountsEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether linked accounts are enabled |
| `announcements` | `object \| null` | No | System announcements |
| `pylonAppId` | `string \| null` | No | Pylon application ID |
| `proxyTemplateUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Proxy template URL |
| `proxyToolboxUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Toolbox template URL |
| `defaultSnapshot` | `string \| null` | No | Default snapshot for sandboxes |
| `dashboardUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Dashboard URL |
| `maxAutoArchiveInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Maximum auto-archive interval in minutes |
| `maintananceMode` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether maintenance mode is enabled |
| `environment` | `string \| null` | No | Current environment |
| `billingApiUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Billing API URL |
| `analyticsApiUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Analytics API URL |
| `v2DashboardUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Base URL of the new Daytona dashboard. The dashboard links to it, with the selected organization id appended, from its "Switch to the new Daytona" entry point when that is enabled for the organization. Absent when the deployment does not advertise one. |
| `stripePublishableKey` | `string \| null` | No | Stripe publishable key for client-side Stripe.js |
| `sshGatewayCommand` | `string \| null` | No | SSH Gateway command |
| `sshGatewayPublicKey` | `string \| null` | No | Base64 encoded SSH Gateway public key |
| `sshGatewayHost` | `string \| null` | No | Hostname of the SSH Gateway that sandbox SSH connections terminate at |
| `sshGatewayPort` | `number \| null` | No | TCP port of the SSH Gateway |
| `sshGatewayHostKeys` | `string[] \| null` | No | SSH host public keys presented by the SSH Gateway, as OpenSSH public key lines (`<type> <base64>`). To build a known_hosts entry, prefix each with `sshGatewayHost` when `sshGatewayPort` is 22, or with `[sshGatewayHost]:sshGatewayPort` otherwise. |
| `sshGatewayHostKeyFingerprints` | `string[] \| null` | No | SHA256 fingerprints of sshGatewayHostKeys, in the same order, in `ssh-keygen -lf` format. Compare against the fingerprint published at https://github.com/daytona/.github/blob/main/SECURITY.md#ssh-host-key-verification |
| `rateLimit` | `object \| null` | No | Rate limit configuration |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="posthog full type">

```ts
{
  apiKey?: string | null,
  host?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="oidc full type">

```ts
{
  issuer?: string | null,
  clientId?: string | null,
  audience?: string | null,
  provider?: string | null,
  authApiHostname?: string | null,
  cli?: {
    issuer?: string | null,
    clientId?: string | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="ssoOidc full type">

```ts
{
  issuer?: string | null,
  clientId?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="announcements full type">

```ts
Record<string, {
  text?: string | null,
  learnMoreUrl?: string | null
}> | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="rateLimit full type">

```ts
{
  failedAuth?: {
    ttl?: number | null,
    limit?: number | null
  } | null,
  authenticated?: {
    ttl?: number | null,
    limit?: number | null
  } | null,
  sandboxCreate?: {
    ttl?: number | null,
    limit?: number | null
  } | null,
  sandboxLifecycle?: {
    ttl?: number | null,
    limit?: number | null
  } | null,
  sandboxList?: {
    ttl?: number | null,
    limit?: number | null
  } | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Docker Registries

### create

`dockerRegistries.create`

Create registry

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.dockerRegistries.create({
	name: 'my-sandbox',
	password: 'example',
	url: 'https://github.com/daytonaio/daytona.git',
	username: 'example',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `mtlsRoleArn` | `string \| null` | No | IAM role ARN for registry mTLS; supply with mtlsSecretArn, or set both to null to clear |
| `mtlsSecretArn` | `string \| null` | No | Secrets Manager ARN for registry mTLS; supply with mtlsRoleArn, or set both to null to clear |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | Registry name |
| `url` | `string` | Yes | Registry URL |
| `username` | `string` | Yes | Registry username |
| `password` | `string` | Yes | Registry password |
| `project` | `string` | No | Registry project |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | Registry ID |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Registry name |
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | Registry URL |
| `username` | `string \| null` | No | Registry username |
| `mtlsRoleArn` | `string \| null \| null` | No | IAM role ARN for registry mTLS |
| `mtlsSecretArn` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Secrets Manager ARN for registry mTLS |
| `project` | `string \| null` | No | Registry project |
| `registryType` | `string \| null` | No | Registry type |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | Creation timestamp |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Last update timestamp |



---

### delete

`dockerRegistries.delete`

Delete registry

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.dockerRegistries.delete({ id: '7a1c3e5f-2b4d-4e6a-8c0b-9d1f3a5c7e9b' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `id` | `string` | Yes | ID of the docker registry |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Daytona accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### getTransientPushAccess

`dockerRegistries.getTransientPushAccess`

Get temporary registry access for pushing snapshots

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.dockerRegistries.getTransientPushAccess({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `regionId` | `string` | No | ID of the region where the snapshot will be available (defaults to organization default region) |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `username` | `string \| null` | No | Temporary username for registry authentication |
| `secret` | `string \| null` | No | Temporary secret for registry authentication |
| `registryUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Registry URL |
| `registryId` | `string \| null` | No | Registry ID |
| `project` | `string \| null` | No | Registry project ID |
| `expiresAt` | `string \| null` | No | Token expiration time in ISO format |

## Object Storage

### getPushAccess

`objectStorage.getPushAccess`

Get temporary storage access for pushing objects

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.objectStorage.getPushAccess({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `regionId` | `string` | No | Region the build context will be used in. When that region has its own build-context bucket, the returned credentials target it; otherwise they target the Daytona-hosted bucket. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accessKey` | `string \| null` | No | Access key for storage authentication |
| `secret` | `string \| null` | No | Secret key for storage authentication |
| `sessionToken` | `string \| null` | No | Session token for storage authentication |
| `storageUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Storage URL |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | Organization ID |
| `bucket` | `string \| null` | No | S3 bucket name |
| `region` | `string \| null` | No | Region for the storage backend (e.g. "us-east-2") |

## Organizations

### createRegion

`organizations.createRegion`

Create a new region

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.organizations.createRegion({ name: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | Region name |
| `proxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Proxy URL for the region |
| `sshGatewayUrl` | `string \| null` | No | SSH Gateway URL for the region |
| `snapshotManagerUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Snapshot Manager URL for the region |
| `otelEndpoint` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint for sandboxes created in this region. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the Daytona-hosted collector and will not be available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. |
| `buildContextBucket` | `string \| null` | No | S3 bucket in your AWS account that declarative-build context is uploaded to for this region. Requires buildContextBucketRegion and buildContextRoleArn. |
| `buildContextBucketRegion` | `string \| null` | No | AWS region of buildContextBucket (standard aws partition only) |
| `buildContextRoleArn` | `string \| null` | No | IAM role Daytona assumes (with your organization ID as ExternalId) to issue upload credentials for buildContextBucket |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the created region |
| `proxyApiKey` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Proxy API key for the region |
| `sshGatewayApiKey` | `string \| null \| null` | No | SSH Gateway API key for the region |
| `snapshotManagerUsername` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Snapshot Manager username for the region |
| `snapshotManagerPassword` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Snapshot Manager password for the region |



---

| `otelEndpoint` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint for sandboxes created in this region. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the Daytona-hosted collector and will not be available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. |
| `buildContextBucket` | `string \| null` | No | S3 bucket in your AWS account that declarative-build context is uploaded to for this region. Requires buildContextBucketRegion and buildContextRoleArn. |
| `buildContextBucketRegion` | `string \| null` | No | AWS region of buildContextBucket (standard aws partition only) |
| `buildContextRoleArn` | `string \| null` | No | IAM role Daytona assumes (with your organization ID as ExternalId) to issue upload credentials for buildContextBucket |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Daytona accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Daytona accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

## Sandboxes

### addNodes

`sandboxes.addNodes`

Append nodes to a multi-node sandbox

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.addNodes({
	sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox',
	expectedGeneration: 1,
	nodes: [{ cpu: 1, disk: 1, memory: 1 }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `expectedGeneration` | `number` | Yes | Current sandbox topology generation. |
| `nodes` | `object[]` | Yes | One resource specification per appended rank. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="nodes full type">

```ts
{
  cpu: number,
  memory: number,
  disk: number
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `phase` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `expectedGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `targetGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `policyRevision` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `completedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### archive

`sandboxes.archive`

Archive sandbox

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.archive({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes of the multi-node GPU sandbox in rank order. Rank 0 is this sandbox. Absent for single-node sandboxes. |
| `topologyGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | Committed logical topology generation. |
| `incarnation` | `number \| null` | No | Physical incarnation for an internal node row. |
| `nodeCount` | `number \| null` | No | Derived number of physical nodes in this sandbox. |
| `nodeRank` | `number \| null` | No | Rank within the multi-node GPU sandbox; present only on internal node rows. |
| `headSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the multi-node GPU sandbox (rank 0); present only on internal node rows. |
| `degraded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The multi-node GPU sandbox remains usable but one or more fabric checks are degraded. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the sandbox |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID of the sandbox |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the sandbox |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | The snapshot used for the sandbox |
| `user` | `string \| null` | No | The user associated with the project |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables for the sandbox |
| `labels` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Labels for the sandbox |
| `public` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox http preview is public |
| `networkBlockAll` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to block all network access for the sandbox |
| `kvm` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox exposes KVM (/dev/kvm) to its guest |
| `networkAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed CIDR network addresses for the sandbox |
| `domainAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed domains for the sandbox |
| `outboundProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Outbound proxy URL the sandbox HTTP(S) traffic is routed through. Applied via the HTTP(S)_PROXY environment variables (convenience routing); network-layer enforcement applies only when the sandbox also has a domainAllowList. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `otelEndpointOverride` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint override for this sandbox. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the default collector and is not available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `target` | `string \| null` | No | The target environment for the sandbox |
| `cpu` | `number \| null` | No | The CPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `gpu` | `number \| null` | No | The GPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `spot` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this is a spot GPU sandbox. Spot sandboxes may be instantly terminated to free capacity for on-demand GPU sandboxes. Absent on APIs that predate this field; treat as false. |
| `spotEvictedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed by spot preemption. Set only for spot-evicted sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after eviction. |
| `queueTimedOutAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed because it waited too long for a runner. Set only for queue-timeout sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after the timeout. |
| `gpuType` | `string \| null` | No | The GPU type assigned to the sandbox |
| `memory` | `number \| null` | No | The memory quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `disk` | `number \| null` | No | The disk quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the sandbox |
| `desiredState` | `string \| null` | No | The desired state of the sandbox |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | The error reason of the sandbox |
| `networkPolicyError` | `string \| null` | No | Outcome of the runner's most recent network policy change: why the network settings fields (what was requested) are not what the runner enforces. Absent when the latest change was applied; a new request leaves it as it is until its own outcome is known. |
| `recoverable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox error is recoverable. |
| `warmPoolId` | `string \| null` | No | Id of the warm pool this sandbox waits in; set only while it is an unclaimed member. |
| `backupState` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the backup |
| `backupCreatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the last backup |
| `autoStopInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-stop interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoPauseInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-pause interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoArchiveInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-archive interval in minutes |
| `autoDeleteInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-delete interval in minutes (negative value means disabled, 0 means delete immediately upon stopping) |
| `autoDestroyAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the sandbox will be automatically destroyed, regardless of its state (only set when a TTL is configured) |
| `queueTimeout` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Minutes to wait for runner assignment before cancelling sandbox creation. Null means the wait is unlimited. |
| `volumes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Array of volumes attached to the sandbox |
| `buildInfo` | `object \| null` | No | Build information for the sandbox |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the sandbox |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last update timestamp of the sandbox |
| `lastActivityAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last activity timestamp of the sandbox |
| `sandboxClass` | `string \| null` | No | The class of the sandbox |
| `daemonVersion` | `string \| null` | No | The version of the daemon running in the sandbox |
| `runnerId` | `string \| null` | No | The runner ID of the sandbox |
| `linkedSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the sandbox this sandbox is linked to. When set, the sandbox is co-located on the same runner as the linked sandbox. |
| `toolboxProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | The toolbox proxy URL for the sandbox |

### create

`sandboxes.create`

Create a sandbox from a snapshot or image; billed per second for its vCPU, memory and disk while it runs

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.create({
	snapshot: 'daytona-small',
	autoStopInterval: 15,
	labels: { team: 'ml' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `name` | `string` | No | The name of the sandbox. If not provided, the sandbox ID will be used as the name |
| `snapshot` | `string` | No | The ID or name of the snapshot used for the sandbox |
| `user` | `string` | No | The user associated with the project |
| `env` | `Record<string, string>` | No | Environment variables for the sandbox |
| `labels` | `Record<string, string>` | No | Labels for the sandbox |
| `public` | `boolean` | No | Whether the sandbox http preview is publicly accessible |
| `networkBlockAll` | `boolean` | No | Whether to block all network access for the sandbox |
| `kvm` | `boolean` | No | Expose KVM (/dev/kvm) inside the sandbox via nested virtualization. linux-vm snapshots only. Requires the sandbox_kvm feature for the organization. |
| `networkAllowList` | `string` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed CIDR network addresses for the sandbox |
| `domainAllowList` | `string` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed domains for the sandbox |
| `outboundProxyUrl` | `string` | No | Outbound proxy URL to route the sandbox HTTP(S) traffic through (http or https; credentials may be included in the URL). On its own this is convenience routing, not a security boundary: it is applied by injecting the standard HTTP(S)_PROXY environment variables at creation, so a process that clears those variables egresses directly. Combine with domainAllowList to have web-port (80/443) egress transparently redirected through the proxy chain at the network layer, which cannot be bypassed from inside the sandbox. |
| `otelEndpointOverride` | `string` | No | OTel collector endpoint override for this sandbox. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the default collector (the Daytona-hosted collector or the region-configured endpoint) and will not be available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. |
| `target` | `string` | No | The target (region) where the sandbox will be created |
| `cpu` | `number` | No | CPU cores allocated to the sandbox. On a multi-node sandbox this scalar applies to every node. |
| `cpuPerNode` | `number[] \| null` | No | CPU cores for each node in rank order. Specify either cpu or cpuPerNode. |
| `gpu` | `number` | No | GPU units allocated to the sandbox |
| `gpuType` | `(H100 \| H200 \| B200 \| B300 \| L40S \| RTX-PRO-6000 \| RTX-4090 \| RTX-5090 \| MI355X)[]` | No | Preferred GPU type for the sandbox. Accepts a single value or an ordered preference list — the scheduler tries each in order and pins the sandbox to the first that has capacity. |
| `spot` | `boolean` | No | GPU-only. When true, the sandbox may be instantly terminated without notice to free GPU capacity for an on-demand (non-spot) GPU sandbox. Ignored / rejected when the sandbox requests no GPUs. |
| `memory` | `number` | No | Memory in GB allocated to the sandbox. On a multi-node sandbox this scalar applies to every node. |
| `memoryPerNode` | `number[] \| null` | No | Memory in GB for each node in rank order. Specify either memory or memoryPerNode. |
| `disk` | `number` | No | Disk space in GB allocated to the sandbox. On a multi-node sandbox this scalar applies to every node. |
| `diskPerNode` | `number[] \| null` | No | Disk space in GB for each node in rank order. Specify either disk or diskPerNode. |
| `autoStopInterval` | `number` | No | Auto-stop interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoPauseInterval` | `number` | No | Auto-pause interval in minutes (0 means disabled). Only supported for sandbox classes that support pausing. Not allowed for ephemeral sandboxes. At most one of autoStopInterval and autoPauseInterval may be non-zero. For non-ephemeral sandbox classes that support pausing, defaults to 60 minutes (with auto-stop disabled) when neither interval is provided. |
| `autoArchiveInterval` | `number` | No | Auto-archive interval in minutes (0 means the maximum interval will be used) |
| `autoDeleteInterval` | `number` | No | Auto-delete interval in minutes (negative value means disabled, 0 means delete immediately upon stopping) |
| `ttlMinutes` | `number` | No | Maximum time to live in minutes, counted as wall-clock time since creation regardless of sandbox state (0 means disabled). When it elapses the sandbox is destroyed, even if it is stopped, paused, or archived. Subject to the maximum sandbox lifespan configured for the organization region and sandbox class, in which case it also defaults to that maximum and cannot be disabled. |
| `queueTimeout` | `number` | No | Minutes to wait for runner assignment before cancelling sandbox creation. Applies only while the sandbox is unassigned in pending_build or pulling_snapshot. Only honored when default queue timeout is enabled for the organization. Omit to use the organization default; null/omit with no organization default leaves the wait unlimited. Must be a positive integer. |
| `volumes` | `object[]` | No | Array of volumes to attach to the sandbox |
| `buildInfo` | `object` | No | Build information for the sandbox |
| `linkedSandbox` | `string` | No | ID or name of an existing sandbox to link the new sandbox to. The new sandbox will be scheduled on the same runner as the linked sandbox so a local network can be established between them. Linked sandboxes must be ephemeral (autoDeleteInterval=0) and cannot themselves be linked to another sandbox. GPU sandboxes cannot participate in links in either direction: a GPU sandbox cannot specify linkedSandbox, and cannot be the link target of another sandbox. |
| `secrets` | `Record<string, string>[]` | No | Secrets to mount in this sandbox. Each entry maps an env var name to a vault secret name. |

```ts
  mountPath: string,
  subpath?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>

```ts
  dockerfileContent: string,
  contextHashes?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes of the multi-node GPU sandbox in rank order. Rank 0 is this sandbox. Absent for single-node sandboxes. |
| `topologyGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | Committed logical topology generation. |
| `incarnation` | `number \| null` | No | Physical incarnation for an internal node row. |
| `nodeCount` | `number \| null` | No | Derived number of physical nodes in this sandbox. |
| `nodeRank` | `number \| null` | No | Rank within the multi-node GPU sandbox; present only on internal node rows. |
| `headSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the multi-node GPU sandbox (rank 0); present only on internal node rows. |
| `degraded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The multi-node GPU sandbox remains usable but one or more fabric checks are degraded. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the sandbox |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID of the sandbox |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the sandbox |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | The snapshot used for the sandbox |
| `user` | `string \| null` | No | The user associated with the project |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables for the sandbox |
| `labels` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Labels for the sandbox |
| `public` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox http preview is public |
| `networkBlockAll` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to block all network access for the sandbox |
| `kvm` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox exposes KVM (/dev/kvm) to its guest |
| `networkAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed CIDR network addresses for the sandbox |
| `domainAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed domains for the sandbox |
| `outboundProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Outbound proxy URL the sandbox HTTP(S) traffic is routed through. Applied via the HTTP(S)_PROXY environment variables (convenience routing); network-layer enforcement applies only when the sandbox also has a domainAllowList. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `otelEndpointOverride` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint override for this sandbox. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the default collector and is not available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `target` | `string \| null` | No | The target environment for the sandbox |
| `cpu` | `number \| null` | No | The CPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `gpu` | `number \| null` | No | The GPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `spot` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this is a spot GPU sandbox. Spot sandboxes may be instantly terminated to free capacity for on-demand GPU sandboxes. Absent on APIs that predate this field; treat as false. |
| `spotEvictedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed by spot preemption. Set only for spot-evicted sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after eviction. |
| `queueTimedOutAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed because it waited too long for a runner. Set only for queue-timeout sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after the timeout. |
| `gpuType` | `string \| null` | No | The GPU type assigned to the sandbox |
| `memory` | `number \| null` | No | The memory quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `disk` | `number \| null` | No | The disk quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the sandbox |
| `desiredState` | `string \| null` | No | The desired state of the sandbox |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | The error reason of the sandbox |
| `networkPolicyError` | `string \| null` | No | Outcome of the runner's most recent network policy change: why the network settings fields (what was requested) are not what the runner enforces. Absent when the latest change was applied; a new request leaves it as it is until its own outcome is known. |

| `backupCreatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the last backup |

### createSshAccess

`sandboxes.createSshAccess`

Create SSH access for sandbox

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.createSshAccess({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | ID or name of the sandbox |
| `expiresInMinutes` | `number` | No | Expiration time in minutes (default: 60, maximum: 1440) |

| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the SSH access was created |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the SSH access was last updated |
| `sshCommand` | `string \| null` | No | SSH command to connect to the sandbox |

### delete

`sandboxes.delete`

Delete a sandbox and its disk

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.delete({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | ID or name of the sandbox |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes of the multi-node GPU sandbox in rank order. Rank 0 is this sandbox. Absent for single-node sandboxes. |
| `topologyGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | Committed logical topology generation. |
| `incarnation` | `number \| null` | No | Physical incarnation for an internal node row. |
| `nodeCount` | `number \| null` | No | Derived number of physical nodes in this sandbox. |
| `nodeRank` | `number \| null` | No | Rank within the multi-node GPU sandbox; present only on internal node rows. |
| `headSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the multi-node GPU sandbox (rank 0); present only on internal node rows. |
| `degraded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The multi-node GPU sandbox remains usable but one or more fabric checks are degraded. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the sandbox |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID of the sandbox |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the sandbox |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | The snapshot used for the sandbox |
| `user` | `string \| null` | No | The user associated with the project |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables for the sandbox |
| `labels` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Labels for the sandbox |
| `public` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox http preview is public |
| `networkBlockAll` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to block all network access for the sandbox |
| `kvm` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox exposes KVM (/dev/kvm) to its guest |
| `networkAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed CIDR network addresses for the sandbox |
| `domainAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed domains for the sandbox |
| `outboundProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Outbound proxy URL the sandbox HTTP(S) traffic is routed through. Applied via the HTTP(S)_PROXY environment variables (convenience routing); network-layer enforcement applies only when the sandbox also has a domainAllowList. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `otelEndpointOverride` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint override for this sandbox. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the default collector and is not available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `target` | `string \| null` | No | The target environment for the sandbox |
| `cpu` | `number \| null` | No | The CPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `gpu` | `number \| null` | No | The GPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `spot` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this is a spot GPU sandbox. Spot sandboxes may be instantly terminated to free capacity for on-demand GPU sandboxes. Absent on APIs that predate this field; treat as false. |
| `spotEvictedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed by spot preemption. Set only for spot-evicted sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after eviction. |
| `queueTimedOutAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed because it waited too long for a runner. Set only for queue-timeout sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after the timeout. |
| `gpuType` | `string \| null` | No | The GPU type assigned to the sandbox |
| `memory` | `number \| null` | No | The memory quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `disk` | `number \| null` | No | The disk quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the sandbox |
| `desiredState` | `string \| null` | No | The desired state of the sandbox |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | The error reason of the sandbox |
| `networkPolicyError` | `string \| null` | No | Outcome of the runner's most recent network policy change: why the network settings fields (what was requested) are not what the runner enforces. Absent when the latest change was applied; a new request leaves it as it is until its own outcome is known. |
| `recoverable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox error is recoverable. |
| `warmPoolId` | `string \| null` | No | Id of the warm pool this sandbox waits in; set only while it is an unclaimed member. |
| `backupState` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the backup |
| `backupCreatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the last backup |
| `autoStopInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-stop interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoPauseInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-pause interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoArchiveInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-archive interval in minutes |
| `autoDeleteInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-delete interval in minutes (negative value means disabled, 0 means delete immediately upon stopping) |
| `autoDestroyAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the sandbox will be automatically destroyed, regardless of its state (only set when a TTL is configured) |
| `queueTimeout` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Minutes to wait for runner assignment before cancelling sandbox creation. Null means the wait is unlimited. |
| `volumes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Array of volumes attached to the sandbox |
| `buildInfo` | `object \| null` | No | Build information for the sandbox |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the sandbox |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last update timestamp of the sandbox |
| `lastActivityAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last activity timestamp of the sandbox |

| `runnerId` | `string \| null` | No | The runner ID of the sandbox |

### expireSignedPortPreviewUrl

`sandboxes.expireSignedPortPreviewUrl`

Expire signed preview URL for a sandbox port

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.expireSignedPortPreviewUrl({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox', port: 3000, token: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | ID or name of the sandbox |
| `port` | `number` | Yes | Port number to expire signed preview URL for |
| `token` | `string` | Yes | Token to expire signed preview URL for |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Daytona accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### getParent

`sandboxes.getParent`

Get sandbox fork parent

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.getParent({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes of the multi-node GPU sandbox in rank order. Rank 0 is this sandbox. Absent for single-node sandboxes. |
| `topologyGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | Committed logical topology generation. |
| `incarnation` | `number \| null` | No | Physical incarnation for an internal node row. |
| `nodeCount` | `number \| null` | No | Derived number of physical nodes in this sandbox. |
| `nodeRank` | `number \| null` | No | Rank within the multi-node GPU sandbox; present only on internal node rows. |
| `headSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the multi-node GPU sandbox (rank 0); present only on internal node rows. |
| `degraded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The multi-node GPU sandbox remains usable but one or more fabric checks are degraded. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the sandbox |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID of the sandbox |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the sandbox |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | The snapshot used for the sandbox |
| `user` | `string \| null` | No | The user associated with the project |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables for the sandbox |
| `labels` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Labels for the sandbox |
| `public` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox http preview is public |
| `networkBlockAll` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to block all network access for the sandbox |
| `kvm` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox exposes KVM (/dev/kvm) to its guest |
| `networkAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed CIDR network addresses for the sandbox |
| `domainAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed domains for the sandbox |
| `outboundProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Outbound proxy URL the sandbox HTTP(S) traffic is routed through. Applied via the HTTP(S)_PROXY environment variables (convenience routing); network-layer enforcement applies only when the sandbox also has a domainAllowList. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `otelEndpointOverride` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint override for this sandbox. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the default collector and is not available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `target` | `string \| null` | No | The target environment for the sandbox |
| `cpu` | `number \| null` | No | The CPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `gpu` | `number \| null` | No | The GPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `spot` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this is a spot GPU sandbox. Spot sandboxes may be instantly terminated to free capacity for on-demand GPU sandboxes. Absent on APIs that predate this field; treat as false. |
| `spotEvictedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed by spot preemption. Set only for spot-evicted sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after eviction. |
| `queueTimedOutAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed because it waited too long for a runner. Set only for queue-timeout sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after the timeout. |
| `gpuType` | `string \| null` | No | The GPU type assigned to the sandbox |
| `memory` | `number \| null` | No | The memory quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `disk` | `number \| null` | No | The disk quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the sandbox |
| `desiredState` | `string \| null` | No | The desired state of the sandbox |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | The error reason of the sandbox |
| `networkPolicyError` | `string \| null` | No | Outcome of the runner's most recent network policy change: why the network settings fields (what was requested) are not what the runner enforces. Absent when the latest change was applied; a new request leaves it as it is until its own outcome is known. |
| `recoverable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox error is recoverable. |
| `warmPoolId` | `string \| null` | No | Id of the warm pool this sandbox waits in; set only while it is an unclaimed member. |
| `backupState` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the backup |
| `backupCreatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the last backup |
| `autoStopInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-stop interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoPauseInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-pause interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoArchiveInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-archive interval in minutes |
| `autoDeleteInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-delete interval in minutes (negative value means disabled, 0 means delete immediately upon stopping) |
| `autoDestroyAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the sandbox will be automatically destroyed, regardless of its state (only set when a TTL is configured) |
| `queueTimeout` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Minutes to wait for runner assignment before cancelling sandbox creation. Null means the wait is unlimited. |
| `volumes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Array of volumes attached to the sandbox |
| `buildInfo` | `object \| null` | No | Build information for the sandbox |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the sandbox |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last update timestamp of the sandbox |
| `lastActivityAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last activity timestamp of the sandbox |
| `sandboxClass` | `string \| null` | No | The class of the sandbox |
| `daemonVersion` | `string \| null` | No | The version of the daemon running in the sandbox |
| `runnerId` | `string \| null` | No | The runner ID of the sandbox |
| `linkedSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the sandbox this sandbox is linked to. When set, the sandbox is co-located on the same runner as the linked sandbox. |
| `toolboxProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | The toolbox proxy URL for the sandbox |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="nodes full type">

```ts
{
  rank?: number | null,
  id?: string | null,
  state?: string | null,
  incarnation?: number | null,

    rank?: number | null,
    memberId?: string | null,
    incarnation?: number | null,
```

### list

`sandboxes.list`

List sandboxes

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.list({ labels: '{"team":"ml"}' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Pagination cursor from a previous response |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Number of results per page |
| `id` | `string` | No | Filter by ID prefix (case-insensitive) |
| `name` | `string` | No | Filter by name prefix (case-insensitive) |
| `labels` | `string` | No | JSON encoded labels to filter by |
| `includeErroredDeleted` | `boolean` | No | Include results with errored state and deleted desired state |
| `includeWarm` | `boolean` | No | Include unclaimed warm pool sandboxes (excluded by default) |
| `states` | `(creating \| restoring \| destroyed \| destroying \| started \| stopped \| starting \| stopping \| error \| build_failed \| pending_build \| building_snapshot \| unknown \| pulling_snapshot \| archived \| archiving \| resizing \| snapshotting \| forking \| pausing \| paused \| resuming)[]` | No | List of states to filter by. |
| `snapshots` | `string[]` | No | List of snapshot names to filter by |
| `regionIds` | `string[]` | No | List of regions IDs to filter by |
| `sandboxClasses` | `(linux-vm \| container \| windows)[]` | No | List of sandbox classes to filter by |
| `minCpu` | `number` | No | Minimum CPU |
| `maxCpu` | `number` | No | Maximum CPU |
| `minMemoryGiB` | `number` | No | Minimum memory in GiB |
| `maxMemoryGiB` | `number` | No | Maximum memory in GiB |
| `minDiskGiB` | `number` | No | Minimum disk space in GiB |
| `maxDiskGiB` | `number` | No | Maximum disk space in GiB |
| `minGpu` | `number` | No | Minimum GPU |
| `maxGpu` | `number` | No | Maximum GPU |
| `isPublic` | `boolean` | No | Filter by public status |
| `isRecoverable` | `boolean` | No | Filter by recoverable status |
| `createdAtAfter` | `string` | No | Include items created after this timestamp |
| `createdAtBefore` | `string` | No | Include items created before this timestamp |
| `lastEventAfter` | `string` | No | Include items with last event after this timestamp |
| `lastEventBefore` | `string` | No | Include items with last event before this timestamp |
| `autoDestroyAtAfter` | `string` | No | Include items scheduled for auto destroy after this timestamp |
| `autoDestroyAtBefore` | `string` | No | Include items scheduled for auto destroy before this timestamp |
| `sort` | `name \| cpu \| memoryGib \| diskGib \| lastActivityAt \| createdAt` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | List of results for the current page |
| `nextCursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Cursor for the next page of results |

### pause

`sandboxes.pause`

Pause sandbox

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.pause({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | ID or name of the sandbox |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes of the multi-node GPU sandbox in rank order. Rank 0 is this sandbox. Absent for single-node sandboxes. |
| `topologyGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | Committed logical topology generation. |
| `incarnation` | `number \| null` | No | Physical incarnation for an internal node row. |
| `nodeCount` | `number \| null` | No | Derived number of physical nodes in this sandbox. |
| `nodeRank` | `number \| null` | No | Rank within the multi-node GPU sandbox; present only on internal node rows. |
| `headSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the multi-node GPU sandbox (rank 0); present only on internal node rows. |
| `degraded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The multi-node GPU sandbox remains usable but one or more fabric checks are degraded. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the sandbox |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID of the sandbox |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the sandbox |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | The snapshot used for the sandbox |
| `user` | `string \| null` | No | The user associated with the project |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables for the sandbox |
| `labels` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Labels for the sandbox |
| `public` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox http preview is public |
| `networkBlockAll` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to block all network access for the sandbox |
| `kvm` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox exposes KVM (/dev/kvm) to its guest |
| `networkAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed CIDR network addresses for the sandbox |
| `domainAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed domains for the sandbox |
| `outboundProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Outbound proxy URL the sandbox HTTP(S) traffic is routed through. Applied via the HTTP(S)_PROXY environment variables (convenience routing); network-layer enforcement applies only when the sandbox also has a domainAllowList. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `otelEndpointOverride` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint override for this sandbox. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the default collector and is not available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `target` | `string \| null` | No | The target environment for the sandbox |
| `cpu` | `number \| null` | No | The CPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `gpu` | `number \| null` | No | The GPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `spot` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this is a spot GPU sandbox. Spot sandboxes may be instantly terminated to free capacity for on-demand GPU sandboxes. Absent on APIs that predate this field; treat as false. |
| `spotEvictedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed by spot preemption. Set only for spot-evicted sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after eviction. |
| `queueTimedOutAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed because it waited too long for a runner. Set only for queue-timeout sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after the timeout. |
| `gpuType` | `string \| null` | No | The GPU type assigned to the sandbox |
| `memory` | `number \| null` | No | The memory quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `disk` | `number \| null` | No | The disk quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the sandbox |
| `desiredState` | `string \| null` | No | The desired state of the sandbox |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | The error reason of the sandbox |
| `networkPolicyError` | `string \| null` | No | Outcome of the runner's most recent network policy change: why the network settings fields (what was requested) are not what the runner enforces. Absent when the latest change was applied; a new request leaves it as it is until its own outcome is known. |
| `recoverable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox error is recoverable. |
| `warmPoolId` | `string \| null` | No | Id of the warm pool this sandbox waits in; set only while it is an unclaimed member. |
| `backupState` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the backup |
| `backupCreatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the last backup |
| `autoStopInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-stop interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoPauseInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-pause interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoArchiveInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-archive interval in minutes |
| `autoDeleteInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-delete interval in minutes (negative value means disabled, 0 means delete immediately upon stopping) |
| `autoDestroyAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the sandbox will be automatically destroyed, regardless of its state (only set when a TTL is configured) |
| `queueTimeout` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Minutes to wait for runner assignment before cancelling sandbox creation. Null means the wait is unlimited. |
| `volumes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Array of volumes attached to the sandbox |
| `buildInfo` | `object \| null` | No | Build information for the sandbox |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the sandbox |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last update timestamp of the sandbox |
| `lastActivityAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last activity timestamp of the sandbox |
| `sandboxClass` | `string \| null` | No | The class of the sandbox |
| `daemonVersion` | `string \| null` | No | The version of the daemon running in the sandbox |
| `runnerId` | `string \| null` | No | The runner ID of the sandbox |
| `linkedSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the sandbox this sandbox is linked to. When set, the sandbox is co-located on the same runner as the linked sandbox. |
| `toolboxProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | The toolbox proxy URL for the sandbox |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="nodes full type">

```ts

  rank?: number | null,
  id?: string | null,
  state?: string | null,
  incarnation?: number | null,
  failure?: {
    rank?: number | null,
    memberId?: string | null,
    incarnation?: number | null,
    reason?: string | null,
    reportedState?: string | null,
    failedAt?: string | null,
    fencingStatus?: string | null,
    fencingError?: string | null
  } | null,
  cpu?: number | null,
  gpu?: number | null,
  memory?: number | null,
  disk?: number | null,
  fabricAddress?: string | null,
  fabricPrefixLength?: number | null,
  fabricHealthy?: boolean | null,
  fabricUnhealthySince?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

```ts
  volumeId?: string | null,
  mountPath?: string | null,
  subpath?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

### removeNodes

`sandboxes.removeNodes`

Remove the highest ranks from a multi-node sandbox

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.removeNodes({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox', count: 1, expectedGeneration: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `expectedGeneration` | `number` | Yes | Current sandbox topology generation. |
| `count` | `number` | Yes | Number of highest ranks to remove. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `phase` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `expectedGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `targetGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `policyRevision` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `completedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

Applied via the HTTP(S)_PROXY environment variables (convenience routing); network-layer enforcement applies only when the sandbox also has a domainAllowList. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `otelEndpointOverride` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint override for this sandbox. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the default collector and is not available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `target` | `string \| null` | No | The target environment for the sandbox |
| `cpu` | `number \| null` | No | The CPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `gpu` | `number \| null` | No | The GPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `spot` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this is a spot GPU sandbox. Spot sandboxes may be instantly terminated to free capacity for on-demand GPU sandboxes. Absent on APIs that predate this field; treat as false. |
| `spotEvictedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed by spot preemption. Set only for spot-evicted sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after eviction. |
| `queueTimedOutAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed because it waited too long for a runner. Set only for queue-timeout sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after the timeout. |
| `gpuType` | `string \| null` | No | The GPU type assigned to the sandbox |
| `memory` | `number \| null` | No | The memory quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `disk` | `number \| null` | No | The disk quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the sandbox |
| `desiredState` | `string \| null` | No | The desired state of the sandbox |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | The error reason of the sandbox |
| `networkPolicyError` | `string \| null` | No | Outcome of the runner's most recent network policy change: why the network settings fields (what was requested) are not what the runner enforces. Absent when the latest change was applied; a new request leaves it as it is until its own outcome is known. |
| `recoverable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox error is recoverable. |
| `warmPoolId` | `string \| null` | No | Id of the warm pool this sandbox waits in; set only while it is an unclaimed member. |
| `backupState` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the backup |
| `backupCreatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the last backup |
| `autoStopInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-stop interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoPauseInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-pause interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoArchiveInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-archive interval in minutes |
| `autoDeleteInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-delete interval in minutes (negative value means disabled, 0 means delete immediately upon stopping) |
| `autoDestroyAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the sandbox will be automatically destroyed, regardless of its state (only set when a TTL is configured) |
| `queueTimeout` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Minutes to wait for runner assignment before cancelling sandbox creation. Null means the wait is unlimited. |
| `volumes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Array of volumes attached to the sandbox |
| `buildInfo` | `object \| null` | No | Build information for the sandbox |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the sandbox |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last update timestamp of the sandbox |
| `lastActivityAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last activity timestamp of the sandbox |
| `sandboxClass` | `string \| null` | No | The class of the sandbox |
| `daemonVersion` | `string \| null` | No | The version of the daemon running in the sandbox |
| `runnerId` | `string \| null` | No | The runner ID of the sandbox |
| `linkedSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the sandbox this sandbox is linked to. When set, the sandbox is co-located on the same runner as the linked sandbox. |
| `toolboxProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | The toolbox proxy URL for the sandbox |

### revokeSshAccess

`sandboxes.revokeSshAccess`

Revoke SSH access for sandbox

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.revokeSshAccess({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | ID or name of the sandbox |
| `token` | `string` | No | SSH access token to revoke. If not provided, all SSH access for the sandbox will be revoked. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes of the multi-node GPU sandbox in rank order. Rank 0 is this sandbox. Absent for single-node sandboxes. |
| `topologyGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | Committed logical topology generation. |
| `incarnation` | `number \| null` | No | Physical incarnation for an internal node row. |
| `nodeCount` | `number \| null` | No | Derived number of physical nodes in this sandbox. |
| `nodeRank` | `number \| null` | No | Rank within the multi-node GPU sandbox; present only on internal node rows. |
| `headSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the multi-node GPU sandbox (rank 0); present only on internal node rows. |
| `degraded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The multi-node GPU sandbox remains usable but one or more fabric checks are degraded. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the sandbox |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID of the sandbox |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the sandbox |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | The snapshot used for the sandbox |
| `user` | `string \| null` | No | The user associated with the project |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables for the sandbox |
| `labels` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Labels for the sandbox |
| `public` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox http preview is public |
| `networkBlockAll` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to block all network access for the sandbox |
| `kvm` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox exposes KVM (/dev/kvm) to its guest |

### rotateSigningKey

`sandboxes.rotateSigningKey`

Rotate the signing key, invalidating all previously signed URLs

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.rotateSigningKey({ sandboxId: '0f9a3c2e-5d6b-4a1f-9c8e-2b7d4e6a1c3f' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxId` | `string` | Yes | ID of the sandbox |



**Output:** `string`

### setAutoPauseInterval

`sandboxes.setAutoPauseInterval`

Set sandbox auto-pause interval

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.setAutoPauseInterval({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox', interval: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | ID or name of the sandbox |
| `interval` | `number` | Yes | Auto-pause interval in minutes (0 to disable) |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes of the multi-node GPU sandbox in rank order. Rank 0 is this sandbox. Absent for single-node sandboxes. |
| `topologyGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | Committed logical topology generation. |
| `incarnation` | `number \| null` | No | Physical incarnation for an internal node row. |
| `nodeCount` | `number \| null` | No | Derived number of physical nodes in this sandbox. |
| `nodeRank` | `number \| null` | No | Rank within the multi-node GPU sandbox; present only on internal node rows. |
| `headSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the multi-node GPU sandbox (rank 0); present only on internal node rows. |
| `degraded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The multi-node GPU sandbox remains usable but one or more fabric checks are degraded. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the sandbox |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID of the sandbox |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the sandbox |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | The snapshot used for the sandbox |

### setTtl

`sandboxes.setTtl`

Set sandbox TTL

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.setTtl({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox', ttlMinutes: 1 });
```

| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | ID or name of the sandbox |
| `ttlMinutes` | `number` | Yes | Maximum time to live in minutes, re-anchored from the current time (0 to disable). When it elapses the sandbox is destroyed, even if it is stopped, paused, or archived. On a multi-node head, expiry destroys every member and all ephemeral local data |
