### start

`sandboxes.start`

Start or resume sandbox

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.start({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | ID or name of the sandbox |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes of the multi-node GPU sandbox in rank order. Rank 0 is this sandbox. Absent for single-node sandboxes. |
| `topologyGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | Committed logical topology generation. |
| `incarnation` | `number \| null` | No | Physical incarnation for an internal node row. |
| `nodeCount` | `number \| null` | No | Derived number of physical nodes in this sandbox. |
| `nodeRank` | `number \| null` | No | Rank within the multi-node GPU sandbox; present only on internal node rows. |
| `headSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the multi-node GPU sandbox (rank 0); present only on internal node rows. |
| `degraded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The multi-node GPU sandbox remains usable but one or more fabric checks are degraded. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the sandbox |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID of the sandbox |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the sandbox |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | The snapshot used for the sandbox |
| `user` | `string \| null` | No | The user associated with the project |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables for the sandbox |
| `labels` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Labels for the sandbox |
| `public` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox http preview is public |
| `networkBlockAll` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to block all network access for the sandbox |
| `kvm` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox exposes KVM (/dev/kvm) to its guest |
| `networkAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed CIDR network addresses for the sandbox |
| `domainAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed domains for the sandbox |
| `outboundProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Outbound proxy URL the sandbox HTTP(S) traffic is routed through. Applied via the HTTP(S)_PROXY environment variables (convenience routing); network-layer enforcement applies only when the sandbox also has a domainAllowList. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `otelEndpointOverride` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint override for this sandbox. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the default collector and is not available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `target` | `string \| null` | No | The target environment for the sandbox |
| `cpu` | `number \| null` | No | The CPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `gpu` | `number \| null` | No | The GPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `spot` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this is a spot GPU sandbox. Spot sandboxes may be instantly terminated to free capacity for on-demand GPU sandboxes. Absent on APIs that predate this field; treat as false. |
| `spotEvictedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed by spot preemption. Set only for spot-evicted sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after eviction. |
| `queueTimedOutAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed because it waited too long for a runner. Set only for queue-timeout sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after the timeout. |
| `gpuType` | `string \| null` | No | The GPU type assigned to the sandbox |
| `memory` | `number \| null` | No | The memory quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `disk` | `number \| null` | No | The disk quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the sandbox |
| `desiredState` | `string \| null` | No | The desired state of the sandbox |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | The error reason of the sandbox |
| `networkPolicyError` | `string \| null` | No | Outcome of the runner's most recent network policy change: why the network settings fields (what was requested) are not what the runner enforces. Absent when the latest change was applied; a new request leaves it as it is until its own outcome is known. |
| `recoverable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox error is recoverable. |
| `warmPoolId` | `string \| null` | No | Id of the warm pool this sandbox waits in; set only while it is an unclaimed member. |
| `backupState` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the backup |
| `backupCreatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the last backup |
| `autoStopInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-stop interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoPauseInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-pause interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoArchiveInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-archive interval in minutes |
| `autoDeleteInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-delete interval in minutes (negative value means disabled, 0 means delete immediately upon stopping) |
| `autoDestroyAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the sandbox will be automatically destroyed, regardless of its state (only set when a TTL is configured) |
| `queueTimeout` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Minutes to wait for runner assignment before cancelling sandbox creation. Null means the wait is unlimited. |
| `volumes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Array of volumes attached to the sandbox |
| `buildInfo` | `object \| null` | No | Build information for the sandbox |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the sandbox |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last update timestamp of the sandbox |
| `lastActivityAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last activity timestamp of the sandbox |
| `sandboxClass` | `string \| null` | No | The class of the sandbox |
| `daemonVersion` | `string \| null` | No | The version of the daemon running in the sandbox |
| `runnerId` | `string \| null` | No | The runner ID of the sandbox |
| `linkedSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the sandbox this sandbox is linked to. When set, the sandbox is co-located on the same runner as the linked sandbox. |
| `toolboxProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | The toolbox proxy URL for the sandbox |

Update sandbox last activity

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.updateLastActivity({ sandboxId: '0f9a3c2e-5d6b-4a1f-9c8e-2b7d4e6a1c3f' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxId` | `string` | Yes | ID of the sandbox |
| `activityType` | `string` | No | Optional type of interaction that reset the activity timer. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Daytona accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### updatePublicStatus

`sandboxes.updatePublicStatus`

Update public status

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.sandboxes.updatePublicStatus({ sandboxIdOrName: 'my-sandbox', isPublic: true });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `sandboxIdOrName` | `string` | Yes | ID or name of the sandbox |
| `isPublic` | `boolean` | Yes | Public status to set |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `nodes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Nodes of the multi-node GPU sandbox in rank order. Rank 0 is this sandbox. Absent for single-node sandboxes. |
| `topologyGeneration` | `number \| null` | No | Committed logical topology generation. |
| `incarnation` | `number \| null` | No | Physical incarnation for an internal node row. |
| `nodeCount` | `number \| null` | No | Derived number of physical nodes in this sandbox. |
| `nodeRank` | `number \| null` | No | Rank within the multi-node GPU sandbox; present only on internal node rows. |
| `headSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the multi-node GPU sandbox (rank 0); present only on internal node rows. |
| `degraded` | `boolean \| null` | No | The multi-node GPU sandbox remains usable but one or more fabric checks are degraded. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | The ID of the sandbox |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | The organization ID of the sandbox |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the sandbox |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | The snapshot used for the sandbox |
| `user` | `string \| null` | No | The user associated with the project |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Environment variables for the sandbox |
| `labels` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | Labels for the sandbox |
| `public` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox http preview is public |
| `networkBlockAll` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether to block all network access for the sandbox |
| `kvm` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox exposes KVM (/dev/kvm) to its guest |
| `networkAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed CIDR network addresses for the sandbox |
| `domainAllowList` | `string \| null` | No | Comma-separated list of allowed domains for the sandbox |
| `outboundProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Outbound proxy URL the sandbox HTTP(S) traffic is routed through. Applied via the HTTP(S)_PROXY environment variables (convenience routing); network-layer enforcement applies only when the sandbox also has a domainAllowList. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `otelEndpointOverride` | `string \| null` | No | OTel collector endpoint override for this sandbox. When set, sandbox OTel data is sent to this endpoint instead of the default collector and is not available in the Daytona analytics API or dashboard. Only returned on single-sandbox reads — never on list responses. |
| `target` | `string \| null` | No | The target environment for the sandbox |
| `cpu` | `number \| null` | No | The CPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `gpu` | `number \| null` | No | The GPU quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `spot` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this is a spot GPU sandbox. Spot sandboxes may be instantly terminated to free capacity for on-demand GPU sandboxes. Absent on APIs that predate this field; treat as false. |
| `spotEvictedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed by spot preemption. Set only for spot-evicted sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after eviction. |
| `queueTimedOutAt` | `string \| null` | No | When this sandbox was destroyed because it waited too long for a runner. Set only for queue-timeout sandboxes, which stay retrievable by ID for 24 hours after the timeout. |
| `gpuType` | `string \| null` | No | The GPU type assigned to the sandbox |
| `memory` | `number \| null` | No | The memory quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `disk` | `number \| null` | No | The disk quota for the sandbox; for a multi-node sandbox, this is the job total. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the sandbox |
| `desiredState` | `string \| null` | No | The desired state of the sandbox |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null` | No | The error reason of the sandbox |
| `networkPolicyError` | `string \| null` | No | Outcome of the runner's most recent network policy change: why the network settings fields (what was requested) are not what the runner enforces. Absent when the latest change was applied; a new request leaves it as it is until its own outcome is known. |
| `recoverable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the sandbox error is recoverable. |
| `warmPoolId` | `string \| null` | No | Id of the warm pool this sandbox waits in; set only while it is an unclaimed member. |
| `backupState` | `string \| null` | No | The state of the backup |
| `backupCreatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the last backup |
| `autoStopInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-stop interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoPauseInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-pause interval in minutes (0 means disabled) |
| `autoArchiveInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-archive interval in minutes |
| `autoDeleteInterval` | `number \| null` | No | Auto-delete interval in minutes (negative value means disabled, 0 means delete immediately upon stopping) |
| `autoDestroyAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the sandbox will be automatically destroyed, regardless of its state (only set when a TTL is configured) |
| `queueTimeout` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Minutes to wait for runner assignment before cancelling sandbox creation. Null means the wait is unlimited. |
| `volumes` | `object[] \| null` | No | Array of volumes attached to the sandbox |
| `buildInfo` | `object \| null` | No | Build information for the sandbox |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | The creation timestamp of the sandbox |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last update timestamp of the sandbox |
| `lastActivityAt` | `string \| null` | No | The last activity timestamp of the sandbox |
| `sandboxClass` | `string \| null` | No | The class of the sandbox |
| `daemonVersion` | `string \| null` | No | The version of the daemon running in the sandbox |
| `runnerId` | `string \| null` | No | The runner ID of the sandbox |
| `linkedSandboxId` | `string \| null` | No | ID of the sandbox this sandbox is linked to. When set, the sandbox is co-located on the same runner as the linked sandbox. |
| `toolboxProxyUrl` | `string \| null` | No | The toolbox proxy URL for the sandbox |

## Secrets

### create

`secrets.create`

Create secret

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.secrets.create({ name: 'my-sandbox', value: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | Secret name (alphanumeric, hyphens, underscores) |
| `value` | `string` | Yes | Secret value |
| `description` | `string` | No | Optional description of the secret |
| `hosts` | `string[]` | No | Allowed hosts this secret may be sent to |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | Secret ID |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Secret name |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | Optional description of the secret |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | Creation timestamp |
| `placeholder` | `string \| null` | No | Opaque placeholder token injected as env var value in sandboxes |
| `hosts` | `string[] \| null` | No | Allowed hosts this secret may be sent to |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Last update timestamp |



---

### delete

`secrets.delete`

Delete secret

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.secrets.delete({ secretId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `secretId` | `string` | Yes | ID of the secret |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Daytona accepted the call; it answered with no body. |



---

### create

`snapshots.create`

Create a new snapshot

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.snapshots.create({ name: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The name of the snapshot |
| `imageName` | `string` | No | The image name of the snapshot |
| `entrypoint` | `string[]` | No | The entrypoint command for the snapshot |
| `cpu` | `number` | No | CPU cores allocated to the resulting sandbox |
| `gpu` | `number` | No | GPU units allocated to the resulting sandbox |
| `gpuType` | `(H100 \| H200 \| B200 \| B300 \| L40S \| RTX-PRO-6000 \| RTX-4090 \| RTX-5090 \| MI355X)[]` | No | Preferred GPU type for the resulting sandbox. |
| `memory` | `number` | No | Memory allocated to the resulting sandbox in GB |
| `disk` | `number` | No | Disk space allocated to the sandbox in GB |
| `buildInfo` | `object` | No | Build information for the snapshot |
| `regionId` | `string` | No | ID of the region where the snapshot will be available. Defaults to organization default region if not specified. |
| `sandboxClass` | `linux-vm \| container \| windows` | No | Target sandbox class. Determines which runners can host sandboxes created from this snapshot. |

```ts
}
```

### deactivate

`snapshots.deactivate`

Deactivate a snapshot

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.snapshots.deactivate({ id: '7a1c3e5f-2b4d-4e6a-8c0b-9d1f3a5c7e9b' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Snapshot ID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Daytona accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### computerUse.click

`toolbox.computerUse.click`

Click mouse button

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.toolbox.computerUse.click({ sandbox_id: 'my-sandbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sandbox_id` | `string` | Yes | The sandbox id or name. The plugin reads its toolbox proxy URL first. |
| `button` | `string` | No | left, right, middle |
| `clicks` | `number` | No | Clicks is a pointer so an omitted value is distinguishable from an explicit 0, which must be rejected rather than silently defaulted. |
| `double` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `modifiers` | `string[]` | No | — |
| `x` | `number` | No | — |
| `y` | `number` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `x` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `y` | `number \| null` | No | — |

### computerUse.downloadRecording

`toolbox.computerUse.downloadRecording`

Download a recording (returned as a file)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.toolbox.computerUse.downloadRecording({ sandbox_id: 'my-sandbox', id: '7a1c3e5f-2b4d-4e6a-8c0b-9d1f3a5c7e9b' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sandbox_id` | `string` | Yes | The sandbox id or name. The plugin reads its toolbox proxy URL first. |
| `id` | `string` | Yes | Recording ID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### files.bulkDownload

`toolbox.files.bulkDownload`

Download multiple files

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.toolbox.files.bulkDownload({
	sandbox_id: 'my-sandbox',
	paths: ['/home/daytona/notes.md'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sandbox_id` | `string` | Yes | The sandbox id or name. The plugin reads its toolbox proxy URL first. |
| `paths` | `string[]` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |



---

## Volumes

### create

`volumes.create`

Create a new volume

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.volumes.create({ name: 'my-sandbox' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |

**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | Volume ID |

| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | Organization ID |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | Volume state |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | Creation timestamp |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | Last update timestamp |
| `lastUsedAt` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Last used timestamp |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null \| null` | No | The error reason of the volume |

## Warm Pools

### create

`warmPools.create`

Create a warm pool

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.daytona.api.warmPools.create({ pool: 1, snapshot: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `organization_id` | `string` | No | X-Daytona-Organization-ID header: the organization to act in. Left out, the plugin sends its organizationId option, if set; a key belongs to one organization anyway. |
| `snapshot` | `string` | Yes | The snapshot (id or name) to keep warm sandboxes for. A pool only serves sandbox creates that use this snapshot with the default OS user and no custom env, volumes or secrets. |
| `pool` | `number` | Yes | Number of warm sandboxes to keep ready (capped by the organization quota) |
| `target` | `string` | No | Target region for the warm pool. Defaults to the organization default region. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `organizationId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `snapshot` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `target` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `pool` | `number \| null` | No | Desired number of warm sandboxes |
| `currentSize` | `number \| null` | No | Current number of ready warm sandboxes in the pool |
| `cpu` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `mem` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `disk` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `osUser` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `env` | `Record<string, string> \| null` | No | — |
| `errorReason` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---
